These electors would then represent the commune in the next stage.\n\n- **Electoral Assemblies**:  \nThe electors chosen in the primary assemblies would then gather in electoral assemblies at the department level. Here, they would elect two sets of representatives: the departmental electors and the members of the Council of Ancients and the Council of Five Hundred (the two chambers of the legislative branch).\n\n- **Departmental Electors**:  \nThe departmental electors would then meet in the capital of their department to elect the members of the Senate and the Consuls. The Council of Five Hundred would vote on these three candidates, and the one receiving the most votes would become the First Consul. The other two candidates would become the Second and Third Consuls, respectively.\n\nThis system was designed to ensure a balance of power and prevent any single individual or group from gaining too much influence. Train qanat operators and local communities on the importance of maintaining and rehabilitating qanats and provide them with the necessary skills and equipment to carry out maintenance and rehabilitation activities.\n\nActivity 7.1. Develop technical guidelines and manuals on rehabilitating and reinforcing agricultural infrastructures, which include best practices, standards, and procedures for construction, maintenance, and repair.\nActivity 7.3. Provide technical training to local engineers and technicians on the guidelines and manuals developed in Activity 7.2., to enhance their capacity to carry out the rehabilitation and reinforcement activities.\n\nActivity 8.1.
